Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the galactic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 32. The moduli space has dimension at most 968 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to galactic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 32/968 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the galactic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 313 and the convergence rate is O(698/n^2).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 313 + O(1/n^698). Summing over all modes and applying the galactic structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
